# Bloblang parity with Bento 1.22.0

Generated by `scripts/parity/regen.py build` from joinedcontext-deployment `cee6ebcb9c8e76dc24f3b4760d24da8ce86228ab`
(components/context-gateway/seed/**/*-bento.yaml). Inputs are the documents the deployment's own Bento tests feed
each mapping; a case is identical when ekiben's stdout and exit code equal Bento's byte for byte.
A text is identical when every case that runs it is, different when one of them differs (the
difference may sit in another step of that case), not runnable when no case runs it.

- Bloblang texts: 4546
- identical: 1626, different: 25, not runnable: 2895
- identical of runnable: 98.5 %
- cases: 904 (620 identical, 2 different, 282 not runnable)
